# ---- E0001 Route103 cap=14 Opening rival battle
## E0001 TRAINER_BRENDAN_ROUTE_103_MUDKIP class=rival
ai: Prefer Highest Damage Move
# Rival / single
plan: The rival's species is resolved from the player's starter generation and choice. The Hoenn branches are level 13 Life Orb physical attackers with matching STAB and coverage: Treecko and Torchic can set up, while Mudkip attacks immediately. Other generations receive a legal species preset from the opponent catalog at runtime.
crack: This is a one-on-one with no hidden reserve. Focus Sash, Eviolite, Leftovers, priority, status, setup denial, Counter or Mirror Coat, Endeavor, recovery, and direct coverage all provide different solutions across the starter families.
TREECKO @LIFE_ORB OVERGROW JOLLY PS -1 | SEED_BOMB, DRAIN_PUNCH, SWORDS_DANCE, QUICK_ATTACK

# ---- E0002 Route102 cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0002 TRAINER_CALVIN_1 class=regular
plan: Poochyena threatens Sucker Punch while Zigzagoon Belly Drums into Extreme Speed; Helping Hand boosts the boosted priority or Guts Taillow's finishing attacks. The reserves extend that opening: Starly supplies Brave Bird.
crack: Protect or Fake Out the Drum turn, hit Zigzagoon at half HP with anything, and answer Taillow with Rock or Electric; Intimidate ruins the whole plan.

# ---- E0003 Route102 cap=14 Stone Badge
## E0003 TRAINER_RICK class=regular
plan: Spinarak slows both active opponents with Electroweb or a single target with Toxic Thread, supporting Sturdy Dwebble and No Guard Karrablast immediately. The reserves extend that opening: Dewpider supplies Liquidation.
crack: Ground blocks Electroweb, Taunt blocks Toxic Thread, and special Fire pressure breaks the bugs without caring about their physical bulk.

# ---- E0027 RustboroCity_Gym cap=14 Stone Badge

plan: Josh guards the door with a wall of Sturdy: Bonsly and Shieldon refuse to fall in one hit and answer with Counter, Rolycoly spins hazards away and rides Steam Engine, Nacli Recovers, and Alolan Geodude uses partner-safe Electric and Ground coverage.

plan: Carbink establishes Trick Room behind Sturdy and Mental Herb.
